bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-11-### Summary
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-12-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-13-This protocol provides a mechanism for the EFI firmware to build and update OS
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-14-configuration data:
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-15-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-16-- device tree (select components with which to build the final one)
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-17-- bootconfig (append fixups)
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-18-- FIT configuration (select the configuration corresponding to the platform)
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-19-- kernel command line (append fixups)
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-20-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-21-GBL will load and verify the data provided by boot partitions, and then call
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-22-these protocol functions to give the firmware a chance to construct and adjust
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-23-the data as needed for the particular device. Device tree fixups are handled by
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-24-the `EFI_DT_FIXUP` protocol.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-25-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-26-If no runtime modifications are necessary, this protocol may be left
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-27-unimplemented, in which case GBL autoselection logic will be used. Refer to
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-28-[`SelectDeviceTrees()`][select_device_trees] description for more details.

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-533-| `EFI_SUCCESS`           | Fixup was successfully written.                                                         |
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_os_configuration_protocol.md-534-| `EFI_UNSUPPORTED`       | No fixup is provided; the kernel command line built by GBL will be used as-is.          |

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-7-2. **Boot Mode & Slot Selection**: GBL reads the bootloader control
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-8-   block (BCB) in the `misc` partition and the platform one-shot boot mode
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-9-   (`GBL_EFI_BOOT_CONTROL_PROTOCOL`) to decide whether to enter Fastboot,
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-10-   Recovery, or Normal boot, and gets the current A/B slot from
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-11-   `GBL_EFI_BOOT_CONTROL_PROTOCOL`.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-12-3. **Load & Verify**:
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-13-   - Loads `boot`, `init_boot`, `vendor_boot`, `vendor_kernel_boot`, `dtb`,
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-14-     `dtbo`, `pvmfw` (when AVF is supported), and any platform-specific
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-15-     partitions requested via `GBL_EFI_AVB_PROTOCOL` into firmware-designated
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-16-     buffers (`GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL`).
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-17-   - Verifies loaded partitions via Android Verified Boot (`libavb` and
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-18-     `GBL_EFI_AVB_PROTOCOL`), enforcing lock state and rollback index updates.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md:19:   - Selects device tree components via `GBL_EFI_OS_CONFIGURATION_PROTOCOL`,
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-20-     applies the selected overlays, and applies final FDT fixups via
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-21-     `EFI_DT_FIXUP_PROTOCOL`.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-22-   - Assembles kernel command line and `bootconfig` parameters (including AVB,
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-23-     slot, and AVF parameters) and applies platform fixups via
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md:24:     `GBL_EFI_OS_CONFIGURATION_PROTOCOL`.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-25-   - When AVF is enabled, builds the `pvmfw` configuration (a DICE handover
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-26-     derived from the vendor DICE handover and a VM reference device tree
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-27-     containing the Secretkeeper public key) via `GBL_EFI_AVF_PROTOCOL`.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-28-4. **Kernel Handoff**: GBL calls
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-29-   `GBL_EFI_BOOT_CONTROL_PROTOCOL.HandleLoadedOs()` when implemented, then exits
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_android_flow.md-30-   UEFI Boot Services and jumps to the kernel entry point.

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-88-If GBL has entered fastboot mode in the same session, preloaded partition data
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-89-may be outdated since new images may have been flashed and active slot may have
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-90-been changed. In this case, GBL calls
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-91-[GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.SyncPartitionBuffer()][sync_partition_buffer] with
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-92-parameter `SyncPreloaded` set to true after exiting fastboot mode and before
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-93-querying any partition buffer. Firmware should either reload the partitions, or
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-94-simply clear the `PRELOADED` bit to instruct GBL to read from disk by itself.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-95-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-96-After GBL finishes assembling kernel/ramdisk/FDT from partition images, and
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-97-before fixing up FDT/bootconfig via
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md:98:[GBL_EFI_OS_CONFIGURATION_PROTOCOL][gbl_os_configuration_protocol], GBL calls
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-99-[GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.SyncPartitionBuffer()][sync_partition_buffer] with
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-100-parameter `SyncPreloaded` set to false. Firmware can take this chance to inspect
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-101-newly loaded images in the provided partition buffers and determines the needed
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-102-FDT/bootconfig fixup if needed.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-103-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-104-The following summarizes the order of events discussed above.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-105-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-106-1. If GBL previously entered fastboot, calls
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-107-   [GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.SyncPartitionBuffer(SyncPreloaded=TRUE)][sync_partition_buffer]
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-108-2. Queries partition buffer with
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-109-   [GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.GetPartitionBuffer()][get_partition_buffer].
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-110-3. Reads, verifies partition images and assembles kernel/ramdisk/FDT.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-111-4. Calls
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-112-   [GBL_EFI_BOOT_MEMORY_PROTOCOL.SyncPartitionBuffer(SyncPreloaded=FALSE)][sync_partition_buffer].
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-113-5. Fixes up FDT and bootconfig via DT_FIXUP and
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md:114:   [GBL_EFI_OS_CONFIGURATION_PROTOCOL][gbl_os_configuration_protocol].
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-115-6. Boot.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-116-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-117-## AARCH64 Kernel Decompression
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-118-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-119-GBL can detect and handle compressed kernel for aarch64. However, current
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-120-implementation requires allocating a separate piece of memory for storing
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-121-decompressed kernel temporarily. This buffer is allocated via EFI memory
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-122-allocation.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-123-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-124-## AVB
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-125-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-126-The AVB (Android Verified Boot) implementation in GBL requires allocating
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-127-additional memory for constructing commandline argument strings and loading
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-128-vbmeta images from disk and any other vendor required partitions for
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_buffer_usage.md-129-verification. The memory is allocated via EFI memory allocation.

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-82-`@gbl//efi/...` is the target to generate rust project for, here it means
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-83-"everything under @gbl//efi/ directory" . Omitting the target specifier would
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-84-result in analyzing "@/..." , which would most likely fail due to some obscure
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-85-reason. Should targets get moved around in the future, this path spec also needs
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-86-to be updated.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-87-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-88-After generating `rust-project.json`, you would notice that your IDE still
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-89-doesn't offer auto completion. This is because some source file paths point to
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-90-bazel-output dir, and you are most likely editing source files in
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md:91:`bootable/libbootloader/gbl`. In addition, the generated rust-project.json sets
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-92-"cfg=test" for all targets, which causes certain dependency graph to resolve
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-93-incorrectly. To fix this, run
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-94-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-95-```sh
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md:96:python3 bootable/libbootloader/gbl/rewrite_rust_project_path.py rust-project.json --arch <arch>
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-97-```
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-98-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-99-where `<arch>` is the target architecture of interest and should be one of
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-100-`x86_64`, `aarch64`, `riscv64`. `<arch>` affects intellisense on architecture
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/README.md-101-specific code.

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-8-- **Architecture:** GBL is a UEFI application encapsulating the Android boot
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-9-  process. Its primary goal is to provide a standardized, platform-agnostic
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-10-  bootloader core that reduces code duplication across different hardware
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-11-  platforms.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-12-- **Platform Support:** GBL supports booting both Android and Fuchsia OSes and
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-13-  runs on architectures including x86_64, aarch64, and riscv64.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-14-- **Tech Stack:**
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-15-  - **Rust:** Core logic (safety-critical).
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-16-  - **C:** Hardware interactions and foundational libraries.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-17-  - **Bazel:** Build system.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-18-  - **Clang:** Compiler (via `prebuilts/`).
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md-19-- **Monorepo Boundaries:**
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md:20:  - `bootable/libbootloader/gbl/`: **Core Logic.** The primary development area.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/gemini/root_gemini.md:21:    Most tasks target this path. See `bootable/libbootloader/gbl/GEMINI.md` for

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-311-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-312-### Description
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-313-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-314-Explicitly sets the target boot slot to the one specified by `Idx`. This
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-315-operation performs the following actions on the target slot:
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-316-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md:317:1. Clears any unbootable reason metadata.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-318-2. Resets the number of tries remaining to a device-specific default.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-319-3. Resets the priority to a device-specific default.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-320-4. Ensures the priority of all other slots is lower than that of the target
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-321-   slot.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-322-5. Clears the slot's `Successful` flag.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-323-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-324-All these changes must be visible in subsequent calls to `GetSlotInfo()`.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-325-Depending on device policy (e.g., lock state), explicitly changing the target
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-326-boot slot may be prohibited.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-327-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-328-### Status Codes Returned

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-495-| `EFI_SUCCESS`            | OS images were handled successfully.                                                     |
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md-496-| `EFI_UNSUPPORTED`        | The firmware does not need to handle OS images. GBL continues the boot process.          |

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-1-# A/B Boot Flow in GBL
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-2-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-3-This document explains the A/B boot flows implemented by GBL and its interaction
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-4-with EFI protocol
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md:5:[`GBL_EFI_BOOT_CONTROL_PROTOCOL`](./gbl_efi_boot_control_protocol.md).

bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-11-## Android
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-12-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-13-Both the GBL bootloader and Android OS partitions must be A/B slotted.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-14-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-15-Note: The boot flow supports >2 slots. We use A/B simply as an example for
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-16-illustration.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-17-
--
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-23-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-24-Device has A/B GBL bootloader and A/B Android OS. Vendor firmware makes A/B slot
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-25-decision and boots to the corresponding GBL slot. GBL simply continues to boot
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-26-the same Android OS slot.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-27-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-28-### Boot Flow
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-29-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-30-The boot flow is summarized in the following diagram.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-31-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-32-![Flowchart of a device booting with A/B slots](./resources/ab-bl-ab-android-flow.png)
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-33-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-34-GBL queries the current boot slot by calling
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md:35:[`GBL_EFI_BOOT_CONTROL_PROTOCOL.GetCurrentSlot()`][get_current_slot]. It also
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-36-tracks whether
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md:37:[`GBL_EFI_BOOT_CONTROL_PROTOCOL.SetActiveSlot()`][set_active_slot] has been
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-38-called to change the next active slot to a different slot, i.e. by
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-39-`fastboot set_active`. If it has, GBL considers that the user intends to boot to
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-40-a different slot than the current one and will trigger a reboot. If not, GBL
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-41-proceeds to load and verify the same slot Android OS. If all operations are
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-42-successful, GBL boots from it. Otherwise it triggers a reboot. Note that in this
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-43-flow, vendor firmware is responsible for updating slot metadata such as
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-44-decrementing retry counters before booting GBL.
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-45-
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-46-## Fuchsia
bootable/libbootloader/gbl/docs/gbl_ab_boot_flow.md-47-
